### 2023-11-30 16:50 — CS #236: On paper, successful; inside, empty: The career fulfillment
 dilemma
- From: Career Supplement (by Resume Worded) @cs.resumeworded.com · To: vishnu88varthan@gmail.com @gmail.com
- Good morning. It's Rohan with your latest _Career Supplement_, your personal mentorship email that takes a few minutes to read, but contains stuff that will speed up your career by years. Today’s _Mentor’s Corner _is about a question we rarely ask ourselves: is chasing career success really making us happy? People spend years, even decades, pursuing what they __think__ defines success – a high-paying job, a fancy car, a beautiful house. But once there, they’re left asking: "Is this it?" Ultimately, success does not equal fulfillment. The problem is, we’re not taught how to achieve the latter.
